<tr><td></td><td>(c) An optical density range greater than 0 to 4</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>Note: Density resolution expressed in density units is measured over the optical density range of the instrument.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1537</td><td>Microwave (including millimetric wave) equipment, including parametric amplifiers capable of operating at frequencies over 1 GHz the following: (other than microwave equipment specified in the entries IL1501, IL1517, IL1520 and IL1529 in this Group)—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Rigid and flexible waveguides designed for use at frequencies in excess of 18 GHz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Waveguides having a bandwidth ratio greater than 1.7:1</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Waveguide components, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) directional couplers having a bandwidth ratio greater than 1.7:1 and directivity over the band of 20 dB or more</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) rotary joints capable of transmitting more than one isolated channel or having a bandwidth greater than 5 per cent of the centre mean frequency, except those used in air traffic control equipment carrying combinations of frequencies suitable for secondary surveillance radar antennae co-mounted on a primary radar antenna and which do not have a bandwidth exceeding 5 per cent of the centre mean frequency</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) magnetic, including gyro-magnetic, waveguide components</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(4) diode waveguide components using diodes specified in the entry IL1544 in this Group</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Transverse electromagnetic mode (TEM) devices the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) those using magnetic, including gyro-magnetic, properties;</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) those using diodes specified in the entry IL1544 in this Group</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(e) Transmit Receive (TR) and anti-TR) tubes and specially designed components therefor, except those designed for use in waveguides and which are in normal civil use for ground marine radar and having any of the following characteristics—</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(i) they operate at a peak power not exceeding 3 MW and at a frequency of 1.5 GHz or less;<br>(ii) they operatre at a peak power not exceeding 1.2 MW and at a frequency over the range of 1.5 to 6 GHz;<br>(iii) they operate at a peak power not exceeding 300 kW and at a frequency over the range of 6 GHz to 10.5 GHz.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(f) Assemblies and sub-assemblies in which the isolating base material functions as a dielectric (as used in stripline, microstrip, or slotline) except for those items specifically designed for use in civil television systems to meet ITU standards and using as an isolating material paper base phenolics, glass cloth melamine, glass cloth epoxy resin, polyethylene terephthalate or other isolating material with an operating temperature not exceeding 150°C</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(g) Phased array antennae and sub-assemblies, designed to permit electronic control of beam shaping and pointing and specially designed components therefor including but not limited to duplexers, phase shifters and associated high-speed diode switches</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>duplexers and phase shifters specifically designed for use in civil television systems or in other civil radar or communication systems not specified elsewhere in this Schedule.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(h) Other antennae specially designed for operation at frequencies above 30 GHz having a diameter of less than 1 metre, and specially designed components therefor</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(i) Microwave assemblies and sub-assemblies capable of being used at frequencies above 3 GHz and having circuits fabricated by the same processes used in integrated circuit technology, which include active circuit elements</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(j) Microwave assemblies and sub-assembles which contain band-pass or band-stop filters and are capable of operating at 3 GHz or greater</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except microwave assemblies, sub-assemblies or amplifiers or combinations therefor and technology for the use thereof, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(a) fixed tuned at the time of manufacture to operate only within the ITU satellite broadcasting band from 11.7 to 12.5 GHz;<br>(b) not capable of being retuned to a new frequency band by the user; and<br>(c) specially designed for use with, or in, civil television receivers</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(k) Amplifiers</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(1) parametric or paramagnetic amplifiers having any of the following characteristics—<br>(i)  they are specially designed for medical applications;<br>(ii)  they are specially for use in simple educational devices and operate at industrial, scientific or medical (ISM) frequencies; or<br>(iii)  they have an output power of not more than 10 W and are specially designed for:<br>(a)   industrial or civilian instrusion detection and alarm systems;<br>(b)   traffic or industrial movement control and counting systems;<br>(c)   environmental pollution air or water detection systems;<br>(8) microwave assemblies, sub-assemblies or amplifiers or combinations therefor, having all of the following characteristics and technology for the use thereof—<br>(a)   fixed tuned at the time of manufacture to operate only within the ITU satellite broadcasting band from 11.7 to 12.5 GHz;<br>(b)   not capable of being returned to a new frequency band by the user; and<br>(c)   specially designed for use with, or in, civil television receivers;<br>(d)   simple educational devices</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(l) Modulators using PIN (positive-intrinsic-negative) transistor technology</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>In this entry “simple educational devices” are devices designed for use in teaching basic scientific principles and demonstrating the operation of those principles in educational institutions.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1541</td><td>Cathode-ray tubes having any of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) A resolving power of 32 lines per mm or more, using the shrinking raster method of measurement</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) With travelling wave or distributed deflection structure using delay lines, or incorporating other techniques to minimise mismatch of fast phenomena signals to the deflection structure, except when using segmented plate (sectioned Y-plate) structure</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Incorporating microchannel-plate electron multipliers;</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>cathode-ray tubes having all of the following characteristics—<br>(i) the microchannel plate electron multipliers have a hole pitch of 25 micrometres or more;<br>(ii) the tubes are not ruggedised for military use;<br>(iii) the tubes have a horizontal sweep slower than 200 ns/cm; and<br>(iv) the electron gun is mounted parallel to the screen surface.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Technology for the design or production of microchannel electron multipliers specified in head (c) above</td><td>D</td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1542</td><td>Cold cathode tubes and switches, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Triggered spark-gaps, having an anode delay time of 15 microseconds or less and rated for a peak current of 3,000 A or more; specially designed parts therefor, and equipment incorporating such devices except cold cathode relay tubes or decade counter tubes</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Cold cathode tubes (other than ignitrons) whether gas-filled or not, operating in a manner similar to a spark gap, containing three or more electrodes and having all the following characteristics—</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) rated for an anode peak voltage of 2,500 volts or more;<br>(2) rated for peak currents of 100A or more;<br>(3) having an anode delay time of 10 microseconds or less; and<br>(4) having an envelope diameter of less than 25.4 mm.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>In this entry “triggered spark-gaps” are tubes with a structure consisting of two opposed anodes with shapes resembling flattened hemispheres, and with one or more triggering probes placed approximately in the centre of one anode. The structure is sealed and contains a mixture of gases, principally nitrogen, under less than atmospheric pressure.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1544</td><td>Semiconductor diodes, the following: and dice and wafers therefor—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Semiconductor diodes, designed or rated for use at input or output frequencies exceeding 12.5 GHz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Mixer and detector diodes designed or rated for use at input or output frequencies greater than 3 GHz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(i) point contact diodes designed or rated for use at input or output frequencies of 12.5 GHz or less;<br>(ii) Schottky diodes designed or rated for mixed use at input or output frequencies of less than 12.5 GHz and having a noise figure of more than 6.5 dB;<br>(iii) Schottky diodes designed or rated for detector use at input or output frequencies of less than 12.5 GHz and having a minimum rated tangential sensitivity of either worse than −45 dBm under unbiased conditions or worse than −50 dBm under biased conditions.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Oscillator and amplifier devices such as Gunn, Impatt, Trapatt, TED and LSA, including those used for the direct conversion of dc to rf power, designed or rated for use at either—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) output frequencies above 1 GHz but not exceeding 4 GHz with a peak power more than 2 W or a maximum CW power more than 200 mW</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) output frequencies above 4 GHz but not exceeding 12.5 GHz with a peak power more than 1 W or a maximum CW power more than 100 mW</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Voltage variable capacitance diodes designed or rated for use at input or output frequencies greater than 1.6 GHz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(e) Fast recovery diodes, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) having a rated maximum reverse recovery time of less than 1 nanosecond</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) having both a rated forward rectified current over 5 A and a rated maximum reverse recovery time of less than 20 nanoseconds</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(f) PIN diodes designed or rated for use at input or output frequencies above 1.7 GHz, with a peak power of greater than 5 W or a maximum CW power of greater than 500 mW</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>There shall be excluded from this entry semiconductor diodes based upon germanium, selenium or copper oxide</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1545</td><td>Transistors, the following and dice and wafers therefor—<br>(a) Transistors based upon silicon and having any of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) an operating frequency exceeding 1.5 GHz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) an operating frequency not exceeding 1.5 MHz and a maximum collector dissipation of more than 300 W</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) an operating frequency exceeding 1.5 MHz and a maximum collector dissipation of more than 250 W</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(4) an operating frequency exceeding 200 MHz and a product of operating frequency (in GHz) times the maximum collector dissipation (in watts) of more than 10; or</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(5) being majority carrier-type transistors, including but not limited to junction field-effect transistors (FETs) and metal-oxide semiconductor transistors (MOS)</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except field-effect transistors having any of the following characteristics—<br>(a) a maximum power dissipation of no more than 6 W and an operating frequency not exceeding 1.0 GHz;<br>(b) a maximum power dissipation of no more than 1 W and an operating frequency not exceeding 2.0 GHz;<br>(c) designed for audio frequency applications</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Transistors based upon gallium arsenide and having any of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) an operating frequency exceeding 1 GHz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) a maximum power dissipation of more than 1 W; or</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) a noise figure of less than 3 dB</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(bb) Technology unique to transistors based upon gallium arsenide</td><td>D</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Transistors based upon any semiconductor material other than germanium, silicon or gallium arsenide</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>In this entry—<br>“maximum collector dissipation” is the continuous dissipation measured under the optimum cooling conditions specified by the manufacture;<br>“operating frequency” is the frequency used in measuring any of the following:<br>(a) output power;<br>(b) power gain (G<sub>pE</sub>, G<sub>pB</sub>, G<sub>pC</sub>, G<sub>pS</sub>, or G<sub>pD</sub>);<br>(c) gain bandwidth product (f<sub>T</sub>); or<br>(d) noise figure</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1547</td><td>Thyristors, the following: and dice and wafers therefor—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Designed for use in pulse modulators having a rated turn-on time of less than 1 microsecond where the rated peak current exceeds 150 A</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Having a rated turn-off time of less than 1 microsecond</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Having a rated turn-off time of from 1 microsecond to less than 2.3 microseconds, except those having a rated peak current of 50 A or less and encapsulated in non-hermetically sealed packages</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Having a rated turn-off time of from 2.3 to 10 microseconds and a figure of merit more than 100</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>In this entry—<br>“figure of merit” is the product of the repetitive peak off-state voltage (vdrm) in kilovolts and the repetitive peak on-state current (1 trm) in amperes as shown on the thyristor data sheets;<br>the “turn-off time” for gate-turn-off thyristors is the sum of the gate controlled time TDQ and the gate controlled fall time Tfq to reach 10 per cent of the initial on-state current.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1548</td><td>Photosensitive components, including linear and focal-plane arrays, the following: and dice and wafers therefor—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Photosensitive components, including photodiodes, phototransistors, photothyristors, photoconductive cells and similar photosensitive components, having either of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) having a peak sensitivity at a wavelength longer than 1,200 nanometres or shorter than 190 nanometres</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) having a peak sensitivity at a wavelength shorter than 300 nanometres and having an efficiency of less than 0.1 per cent relative to peak response at wavelengths longer than 400 nanometres</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except vacuum photodiodes specially designed for use in spectrophotometry having a peak response at a wavelength shorter than 300 nanometres.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Semiconductor photodiodes and phototransistors with a response time constant of 95 ns or less measured at the operating temperature for which the time constant reaches a minimum</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except semiconductor photodiodes which are not space qualified with a response time constant of 0.5 ns or more and with a peak sensitivity at a wavelength neither longer than 920 nm nor shorter than 300 nm</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Photo sensitive components specially designed or rated as electromagnetic, including laser and ionized-particle radiation resistant</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Linear and focal plane arrays (hybrid or monolithic) having the characteristics specified in heads (a)(1) or (2) or (b) above, and specially designed components therefor</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>There shall be excluded from this entry—<br>(a) germanium photo devices with a peak sensitivity at a wavelength shorter than 1,750 nanometres;<br>(b) infrared single-element encapsulated photoconductive cells or pyroelectric detectors intended for civil applications and using any of the following—<br>(1) evaporated lead sulphide;<br>(2) trigylcine sulphate with a surface area of 20 mm2 or less;<br>(3) lead-lanthanum-zirconium titanate ceramic;<br>(c) single-element encapsulated mercury-cadmium-telluride (HgCdTe) uncooled (295 K ambient temperature operation) photo-electro-magnetic (pem) or photoconductive (pc) mode photodetectors with a peak sensitivity at a wavelength shorter than 11,000 nanometres.<br>In this entry—<br>The “time constant” is the time taken from the application of a light stimulus for the current increment to reach a value of 1-1/e times the final value (ie 63 per cent of the final value);<br>“space qualified” means products which are stated by the manufacturer as designed and tested to meet the special electrical, mechanical or environmental requirements for use in rockets, satellites or high-altitudes flight systems operating at altitudes of 100 km or more.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1549</td><td>Photomultiplier tubes, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Those for which the maximum sensitivity occurs at wavelengths shorter than 300 nanometres</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except photomultiplier tubes specially designed for use in spectrophotometry having a peak sensitivity at a wavelength shorter than 300 nanometres.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Those having an anode pulse rise time of less than 1 nanosecond</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Those which contain microchannel-plate electron multipliers</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1553</td><td>Flash discharge type X-ray systems, including tubes, having all of the following characteristics—</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Peak power greater than 500 MW;<br>(b) Output voltage greater than 500 kV;<br>(c) Pulse width less than 0.2 microsecond</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1555</td><td>Electron tubes, the following: and specially designed components therefor—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Electron tubes for image conversion or intensification which incorporate any of the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) fibre optic face plates specified in head (a) of the entry IL1556 in this Group</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) microchannel-plate electron multipliers</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) gallium arsenide or other epitaxially grown semi-conductor photocathodes specified in head (c) to the entry IL1556 in this Group</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(aa) Technology for image intensifiers or converters incorporating fibre-optic plates or microchannel plate electron multipliers, or electron tubes for cameras incorporating such intensifiers or converters</td><td>D</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Electron tubes for television/video cameras, having any of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) incorporating fibre-optic face plates specified in head (a) of the entry IL1556 in this Group</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) incorporating microchannel-plate electron multipliers</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) coupled with electron tubes specified at head (a) above</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Ruggedised electron tubes for television/video cameras having a maximum length-to-bulb diameter ratio of 5:1 or less</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>There shall be excluded from this entry—<br>commercial standard television/video camera tubes not incorporating fibre-optic plates specified in head (a) of the entry IL1556;<br>commercial standard X-ray amplifier tubes.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1556</td><td>Optical elements and elements for optical tubes, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Non-flexible fused fibre-optic plates or bundles, having all of the following characteristics—</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) a fibre pitch (centre-to-centre spacing) of less than 10 micrometres;<br>(2) a light-absorbing medium surrounding each fibre, or interstitially placed between fibres; and<br>(3) a diameter greater than 13 mm;</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Microchannel-plates for electron image amplification, having both of the following characteristics—</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) 15,000 or more hollow tubes per plate; and<br>(2) hole pitch (centre-to-centre spacing) of less than 25 micro-metres</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Semi-transparent photocathodes incorporating epitaxially grown layers of compound semiconductors, such as gallium arsenide</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Diffractive type optical elements specially designed for display screens, with any of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) a transmission of more than 90 per cent outside the reflection band and reflection of more than 75 per cent inside the reflection band, which has less than 15 nanometres bandwidth and is matched to the frequency of the display light source</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) a rear projection screen brightness gain of more than 10 times the gain of a Lambertian scatterer with an equivalent area, and less than 10 per cent variation in brightness across the exit aperture</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) specially designed for use in helmet-mounted displays</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1558</td><td>Electronic vacuum tubes (valves) and cathodes, the following: and other components specially designed for those tubes—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Tubes in which space charge control is utilized as the primary functional parameter, including triodes and tetrodes, the following</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) tubes rated for continuous wave operation having either of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(i) those above 4 GHz at maximum rated anode dissipation</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(ii) those within the frequency range 0.3 to 4 GHz and for which, under any condition of cooling, the product of the maximum rated anode dissipation, expressed in watts, and the square of the maximum frequency expressed in GHz, at the maximum rated anode dissipation is greater than 10<sup>4</sup>; except tubes specially designed for television transmitters operating in the frequency range of 0.047 to 0.96 GHz and rated for operation without a grid current, for which the product of the rated anode dissipation, expressed in watts, and the square of the maximum frequency, expressed in GHz, may reach 2 × 10<sup>4</sup></td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) tubes rated only for pulse operation having either of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(i) above 1 GHz with maximum peak pulse output power greater than 45 kW</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(ii) between 0.3 and 1 GHz and for which, under any condition of cooling, the product of the peak pulse output power, expressed in watts, and the square of the maximum frequency, expressed in GHz, is greater than 4.5 × 10<sup>4</sup></td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(3) tubes specially designed for use as pulse modulators for radar or similar applications, having a peak anode voltage rating of 100 kV or more, or rated for a peak pulse power of 20 MW or more</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Tubes which utilize interaction between a beam of electrons and microwave elements and in which the electrons travel in a direction perpendicular to the applied magnetic field, including but not limited to magnetrons, crossed-field amplifier tubes and crossed-field oscillator tubes</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(i) fixed frequency and tunable pulsed magnetrons and crossfield amplifier tubes which are in normal civil use, the following—<br>(1)  magnetrons designed to operate at frequencies below 3 GHz with a maximum rated peak output power of 5 MW or less, or between 3 to 12 GHz with the product of the maximum rated peak output power, expressed in kilowatts, and the frequency, expressed in Giga-hertz, less than 4,200 and a frequency tuning time of more than 100 milliseconds;<br>(2)  crossed-field amplifier tubes designed to operate at frequencies below 4 GHz with a maximum rated average output power of 1.2 kW or less, a bandwidth of 200 MHz or less and a gain of less than 15 dB;<br>(ii) fixed frequency continuous wave magnetrons designed for medial use or for industrial heating or cooking purposes operating at a frequency of 2.375 GHz ±0.05 GHz or 2.45 GHz ±0.05 GHz with a maximum rated output power not exceeding 6 kW or, at a frequency lower than 1 GHz, with a maximum rated output power not exceeding 35 kW;<br>(iii) magnetrons, specially designed for particle accelerators for medical radiation therapy, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1)  capable of operation only at a frequency of 3,000 MHz ±15 MHz or at a frequency of 2,856 MHz ±15 MHz;<br>(2)  not capable of being tuned mechanically or electronically outside the above bands;<br>(3)  mechanically tuned within the above bands;<br>(4)  having a peak output power not exceeding 10 MW and an average output power not exceeding 15 kW.<br>The exception in paragraph (iii) above does not apply to technological documents the information in which relates to goods excluded in paragraph (iii).</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Tubes which utilise interaction between a beam of electrons and microwave elements or cavities and in which the electrons travel in a direction parallel to the applied magnetic field (including Klystrons or travelling wave tubes)</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(i) continuous wave tubes having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1) designed for use in civil ground communication;<br>(2) an instantaneous bandwidth of half an octave or less, ie the highest operating frequency is not higher than 1.5 times the lower operating frequency;<br>(3) the product of the rated output power (expressed in W) and the maximum operating frequency (expressed in GHz) of no more than 300;<br>(4) an operating frequency no higher than 20 GHz;<br>(5) no multiple grid electron guns; and<br>(6) collectors with no more than two depressed stages;<br>(ii) pulsed tubes, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1) for civil applications<br>(2) an instantaneous bandwidth of half an octave or less ie the highest operating frequency is not higher than 1.5 times the lowest operating frequency;<br>(3) collectors with no more than two depressed stages; and<br>(4) either of the following:<br>(a)  a peak saturated output power not exceeding 1 kW, an average output power not exceeding 40 W and the operating frequency not exceeding 10 GHz;<br> or<br>(b)  a peak saturated output not exceeding 100 W, an average output power not exceeding 20 W and the operating frequency between 10 and 20 GHz;<br>(iii) pulsed tubes, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1) for civil applications;<br>(2) designed for fixed frequency operation;<br>(3) operating frequencies below 3.5 GHz;<br>(4) a peak output power of 1.6 MW or less; and<br>(5) an operating bandwidth of less than 1 per cent;<br>(iv) tubes, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1) used as fixed-frequency or voltage-tunable oscillator tubes;<br>(2) designed to operate at frequencies below 20 GHz; and<br>(3) a maximum output power of less than 3 W;<br>(v) klystrons specially designed for particle accelerators for medical radiation therapy, having all the following characteristics—<br>(1) capable of operation only at a frequency of 3,000 MHz ±15 MHz or at a frequency of 2,856 MHz ±15 MHz;<br>(2) not capable of being tuned mechanically or electronically outside the above bands;<br>(3) mechanically tuned within the above bands;<br>(4) having a peak output power not exceeding 10 MW and an average output power not exceeding 15 kW.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>The exception in paragraph (v) above does not apply to technological documents the information in which relates to goods excluded in paragraph (v).</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Tubes which utilize interaction between an electron beam and microwave elements or cavities but do not require a magnetic field to control or focus the electron beam, except low power reflex oscillator klystrons designed to operate at frequencies below 20 GHz and at a maximum output power of less than 3 W</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(e) Tubes which utilize interaction between a beam of electrons and microwaves elements or cavities in which the electrons drift in a direction parallel to the applied magnetic field but also require for their operation a large component of velocity transverse to the direction of the applied magnetic field, including gyrotrons, ubitrons and peniotrons</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(f) Tubes designed to withstand on any axis an acceleration of short duration (shock) greater than 1,000 g</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(g) Tubes designed for operation in ambient temperatures exceeding 200°C</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(h) Tubes of the type specified in heads (c), (d) or (e) above, which are designed to operate with no filament or cathode heating element as indicated by the absence of heating supply connections</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(i) Tubes which utilize a modulated bean of electrons striking one or more semiconductor diodes to provide power gain</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(j) Cathodes for electronic vacuum tubes, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) those specially designed for tubes specified in heads (a) to (i) above (inclusive)</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>or</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) impregnated cathodes capable of producing a current density exceeding 0.5 A/cm<sup>2</sup> at rated operating conditions</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>In this entry—<br>“frequency tuning time” is the time required to change the operating frequency from a starting frequency, through the maximum frequency, through the minimum frequency, and return to the starting frequency, ie one complete tuning cycle. (“Frequency tuning time”:<br><a href="https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/1989/2376">formula: see source</a></td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1559</td><td>Hydrogen/hydrogen isotope thyratons of ceramic-metal construction and having any of the following characteristics: and accessories therefor—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) A peak pulse power output exceeding 12.5 MW</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) A peak anode voltage greater than 25 kV</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) A peak current rating greater than 1.5 kA</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>Note. For thyratrons rated for both single-shot (crowbar) and modulator service, the figure for modulator service should be used.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1560</td><td>Capacitors designed for or capable of maintaining their rated electrical and mechanical characteristics during their specified operating lifetime, and technology therefor, the following—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Monolithic ceramic capacitors (other than boundary layered capacitors) using non-ferro-electric strontium titanate (SrTiO3) dielectric rated for operation over the whole range of ambient temperatures from below −55°C to above +85°C</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Technology for the design and production of tantalum capacitors rated for operation at ambient temperatures exceeding 125°C, except sintered electrolytic types having a casing made of epoxy resin or which are sealed or coated with opoxy resin</td><td>D</td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1561</td><td>Materials specially designed and manufactured for use as absorbers of electromagnetic waves having frequencies greater than 2 × 10<sup>8</sup> Hz and less than 3 × 10<sup>12</sup> Hz</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except, save when contained in paint,<br>(i) hair type absorbers, whether constructed of natural or synthetic fibres, with non-magnetic loading to provide absorption;<br>(ii) absorbers whose incident surface is non-planar in shape, including pyramids, cones, wedges and convoluted surfaces, and which have no magnetic loss; and<br>(iii) absorbers having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1) they are made of:<br>(a) plastic foam materials (flexible or non-flexible) with carbon-loading to provide absorption; or<br>(b) organic binders with magnetic material loading which do not provide broad-band absorption performance with low reflectivity;<br>(2) the incident surface is planar;<br>(3) their tensile strength is less than 7 × 10<sup>6</sup> N/m<sup>2</sup>; and<br>(4) their compressive strength is less than 14 × 106 N/m<sup>2</sup>; and<br>(5) they cannot withstand more than 450 K (177°C).<br>In this entry—<br>“Broad-band absorption performance with low reflectivity” means less than 5 per cent echo compared with metal over a bandwidth greater than ±15 per cent of the centre frequency of the incident energy.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td>IL1564</td><td>Assemblies of electronic components, modules, printed circuit boards with mounted components, substrates and integrated circuits, including packages therefor, the following—<br>NOTE:<br>Integrated circuits are categorized as follows:<br>monolithic integrated circuits<br>microcomputer microcircuits<br>microprocessor microcircuits<br>multichip integrated circuits<br>film type integrated circuits<br>hybrid integrated circuits<br>optical integrated circuits</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(a) Substrates for printed circuit boards, including ceramic substrates and coated metal substrates (single-sided, double-sided or multilayer), and thin copper foils therefor</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(1) printed circuit boards manufactured from any of the following materials—<br>(a) paper base phenolics;<br>(b) glass cloth melamine;<br>(c) glass epoxy resin uncoated or coated with copper foil of a thickness of 18 micrometres or more;<br>(d) polyethylene terephtahalate; or<br>(e) any other insulating material having all of the following characteristics:<br>(i)  a maximum continuous rated operating temperature not exceeding 423 K (150°C);<br>(ii)  a dissipation factor equal to or more than 0.009 at 1 MHz;<br>(iii)  a relative dielectric constant equal to or less than 8 at 1 MHz; and<br>(iv)  a coefficient of expansion equal to or more than ±10<sup>−5</sup>/K over a temperature range of 273 K to 393 K (0°C to 120°C);<br>(2) ceramic substrates having no more than two layers of interconnections, including the ground plane; or<br>(3) copper foil having a thickness of 18 micrometre or more;</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(b) Ceramic packages for integrated circuits which are designed for hermetically sealed pin or pad grid array, leadless carrier or surface-mounted configurations</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except those having all of the following characteristics—<br>(1) single-in-line, dual-in-line or flat-pack configuration;<br>(2) pin, pad or lead spacings of 2.50 mm or more; and<br>(3) 40 leads or less;</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(c) Assemblies, modules and printed circuit boards with mounted components, with any of the following characteristics—</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(1) those which include substrates for printed circuit boards specified in head (a) above</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(2) those which contain components specified in this Schedule</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(a) where the only components specified in this Schedule which they contain are capacitors;<br>(b) power supply assemblies;<br>(c) non-coherent light-emitting alphanumeric displays, which if incorporated monolithic integrated circuits have both the following characteristics:—<br>(i)  used for decoding, controlling or driving the display; and<br>(ii)  not integral with the actual display device; or<br>(f) simple encapsulated photo-coupler (transopter) assemblies, having both of the following characteristics—<br>(i)  electrical input and output; and<br>(ii)  any incorporated light-emitting diode can only emit non-coherent light;<br>(i) assemblies, modules or printed circuit boards with mounted components, having both of the following characteristics—<br>(i)  designed for equipment not specified elsewhere in this Schedule; and<br>(ii)  substantially restricted to the particular application for which they have been designed by nature of:<br>(1)   design;<br>(2)   performance;<br>(3)   lack of user-accessible microprogrammability;<br>(4)   lack of user-accessible programmability;<br>(5)   software;<br>(6)   microprogramme control; or<br>(7)   specialized logic control.<br>NOTES:<br><br>1. For assemblies, modules or printed circuit boards with mounted components which are designed for, or which have the same functional characteristics as, electronic computers or related equipment, see entry IL1565 in Group 3G.<br>2. This head does not apply to assemblies, modules or printed circuit boards with mounted components which are designed for, or which have the same functional characteristics as, equipment specified elsewhere in this Schedule.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>(d) Monolithic integrated circuits, microcomputer microcircuits, microprocessor microcircuits, multichip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits, hybrid integrated circuits and optical integrated circuits</td><td>C</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>except—<br>(1) encapsulated passive networks save technology for the manufacture of thin film passive networks;<br>(2) encapsulated integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(A) not designed or rated as radiation hardened;<br>(B) not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 233 K (−40°C) or above 358 K (85°C);<br>(C) packaged in any of the following casings:<br> (i)  TO-5 outline cases (diameter 7.7 to 9.4mm);<br> (ii)  hermetically sealed dual in-line cases; or<br> (iii)  non-hermetically sealed cases; and<br>(D) being any of the following types:<br> (a)  bipolar monolithic integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   designed to perform a single digital logic function or a combination of digital logic functions;<br>  (2)   encapsulated in packages having 24 terminals or less;<br>  (3)   a basic gate propagation delay time of no less than 3 ns;<br>  (4)   a basic gate power dissipation of no less than 2 mW; and<br>  (5)   a product of the basic gate propagation delay time and the basic gate power dissipation per gate of no less than 30 pJ for types having a basic gate propagation delay time of 3 ns or more and less than 5 ns;<br> (b)  bipolar monolithic integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   designed for operation in civil applications;<br>  (2)   being either:<br>   (a)    electronic switches, externally controlled by inductive, magnetic or optical means; or<br>   (b)    threshold value switches; and<br>  (3)   with switching times of 0.5 microsecond or more;<br> (c)  complementary metal-oxide semiconductor (CMOS) monolithic integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   designed for operation as digital logic circuit elements but limited to gates, inverters, buffers, flip-flops, latches, multivibrators, bilateral switches, display drivers, fixed counters, fixed frequency dividers, storage registers, decoders, voltage translators, encoders, Schmidt triggers, delay timers, carry generators, clock generators, and any combination of the above digital logic functions;<br>  (2)   encapsulated in packages having 24 terminals or less; and<br>  (3)   a minimum value of the basic gate propagation delay time under any rated condition of no less than 10 ns;<br> (d)  positive-channel type or negative-channel type metal-oxide semiconductor (PMOS or NMOS) monolithic integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   designed for and by virtue of circuit design limited to use as serial digital shift registers;<br>  (2)   a maximum clock rate of 10 MHz; and<br>  (3)   a maximum of 1,024 bit per package;<br> (e)  silicon microcomputer microcircuits (save bit slice microcomputer microcircuits) having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   mask programmed by the manufacturer for a civil application prior to exportation;<br>  (2)   a word size to speed ratio of less than or equal to 1.1 bit per microsecond;<br>  (3)   a speed-power dissipation product of more than or equal to 1.2 microjoule;<br>  (4)   not containing on-the-chip;<br>   (a)    a read-only storage (ROM) of more than 8,192 byte (not including the storage space needed for the micro-programme);<br>   (b)    a random access storage (RAM) of more than 256 byte;<br>   (c)    a programmable read-only storage (PROM);<br>   (d)    multiplication capabilities;<br>   (e)    general purpose operating systems including CP/M; or<br>   (f)    high order languages including Tiny Basic;<br>  (5)   an operand (data) word length of less than or equal to 8 bit;<br>  (6)   not capable of using storage off-the-chip for programme storage; and<br>  (7)   not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br> (f)  silicon monolithic integrated circuits, micro-computer microcircuits, microprocessor micro-circuits, multichip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits, hybrid integrated circuits, or optical integrated circuits, having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   no user-accessible microprogrammability; and<br>  (2)   designed or programmed by the manufacturer for one or more of the following applications:<br>   (a)    car electronics including entertainment, instrumentation, safety, comfort, operations or pollution;<br>   (b)    home electronics, including audio and video equipment, appliances, safety, education, comfort, remote controlled toys or amusement;<br>   (c)    timekeeping applications including watches or clocks;<br>   (d)    personal communications up to 150 MHz, including amateur radio communication and intercom;<br>   (e)    cameras not specified elsewhere in this Schedule including cine cameras but excluding imaging micro-circuits;<br>   (f)    medical electronic prostheses including cardiac pacemakers, hearing aids; or<br>   (g)    civil telephone subscriber sets providing neither ISDN functions nor encryption.<br>NOTE:<br>  The temperature limits specified in exception (2)(B) to head (d) above do not apply to exception (2)(D)(f)(2)(a) or (f) to head (d) above.<br> (g)  monolithic integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   not capable of addressing off-the-chip storage;<br>  (2)   no user-accessible microprogrammability; and<br>  (3)   designed for and by virtue of circuit design limited to use in simple calculators, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    performing a single function in response to a keystroke; and<br>   (b)    capable of performing floating point additions of a maximum of 13 decimal digits (mantissa only) in not less than 20 ms;<br> (h)  monolithic integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   no user-accessible microprogrammability; and<br>  (2)   designed for and by virtue of circuit design limited to use in simple key programmable calculators, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    capable of executing a sequence of no more than 256 programme steps introduced into a programme storage on-the-chip by a sequence of keystrokes; and<br>   (b)    capable of performing floating point additions of a maximum of 13 decimal digits (mantissa only) in not less than 20 ms;<br> (i)  silicon microprocessor microcircuits (save bit slice microprocessor microcircuits), having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   a word size to speed ratio of less than or equal to 1.25 bit per microsecond;<br>  (2)   a speed-power dissipation product of more than or equal to 2 microjoule;<br>  (3)   not containing on-the-chip;<br>   (a)    read-only storage (ROM);<br>   (b)    programmable read-only storage (PROM);<br>   (c)    random-access storage (RAM) of more than 1,024 bit; or<br>   (d)    multiplication instructions;<br>  (4)   capable of addressing storage off-the-chip of no more than 65,536 byte;<br>  (5)   an operand (data) word length of less than or equal to 8 bit;<br>  (6)   an arithmetic logic unit (ALU) not wider than 8 bit; and<br>  (7)   not rated for operation at am ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br> (j)  storage monolithic integrated circuits or multi-chip integrated circuits, the following:<br>  (1)   read-only memory (ROMs), having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    mask programmed by the manufacturer for a civil application prior to exportation;<br>   (b)    a maximum of 8,192 bit per package;<br>   (c)    a maximum access time of no less than 450 ns; and<br>   (d)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br>  (2)   positive-channel type or negative-channel type metal-oxide semiconductor read-only memory (PMOS- or NMOS-ROMs), having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    mask programmed by the manufacturer for a civil application prior to exportation;<br>   (b)    a maximum of 32,768 bit per package;<br>   (c)    a maximum access time of no less than 450 ns; and<br>   (d)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br>  (3)   positive-channel type or negative-channel type metal-oxide semiconductor read-only memory (PMOS- or NMOS-ROMs), having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    mask programmed or designed as character generators for a standard character font;<br>   (b)    a maximum access time of not less than 250 ns; and<br>   (c)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br>  (4)   programmable (non-erasable) read-only memory (PROMs) having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    programmed by the manufacturer for a civil application prior to exportation;<br>   (b)    a maximum of 2,048 bit per package;<br>   (c)    a maximum access time of not less than 250 ns; and<br>   (d)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br>  (5)   programmable (non-erasable) read-only memory (PROMs) having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    programmed by the manufacturer for a civil application prior to exportation;<br>   (b)    a maximum of 8,192 bit per package;<br>   (c)    a maximum access time of not less than 450 ns; and<br>   (d)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br>  (6)   bipolar random-access memory (RAMs), having any of the following pairs of characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum of 64 bit per package and a maximum access time of not less than 30 ns;<br>   (b)    a maximum of 256 bit per package and a maximum access time of not less than 40 ns; or<br>   (c)    a maximum of 1,024 bit per package and a maximum access time of not less than 45 ns;<br>  (7)   metal-oxide-semiconductor dynamic random access memory (MOS-DRAMs), having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum of 4,096 bit per package;<br>   (b)    a maximum access time of not less than 250 ns; and<br>   (c)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 253 K (−20°C) or above 348 K (75°C);<br>  (8)   metal-oxide semiconductor static random access memory (MOS-SRAMs), having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum of 1,024 bit per package; and<br>   (b)    a maximum access time of not less than 340 ns;<br> (k)  amplifier monolithic integrated circuits, multi-chip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, the following:<br>  (1)   audio amplifiers:<br>   (a)    having a maximum rated continuous power output of 50 W or less at am ambient temperature of 298 K (25°C); or<br>   (b)    encapsulated in non-hermetically sealed packages;<br>  (Note: For audio amplifiers, the 358 K (85°C) upper temperature limit specified in exception (2)(B) to head (d) above is not applicable.)<br>  (2)   instrumentation amplifiers, having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a best-case rated linearity of no better than ±0.01 per cent at a gain of 100;<br>   (b)    a maximum gain-bandwidth product of no more than 7.5 expressed in MHz (eg a maximum bandwidth of 75 kHz at −3 dB and a gain of 100); and<br>   (c)    a typical slew rate at unity-gain not exceeding 3 V/microsecond;<br>  (3)   isolation amplifiers;<br>  (4)   operational amplifiers, having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a typical unity-gain open-loop bandwidth of no more than 5 MHz;<br>   (b)    a typical open-loop voltage gain of no more than 10<sup>6</sup>, ie 120 dB;<br>   (c)    either:<br>    (i)     a maximum intrinsic rated input offset voltage of no less than 1.0 mV; or<br>    (ii)     a maximum input offset voltage drift of no less than 5 microvolt/K;<br>   (d)    a typical slew rate at unity-gain not exceeding 6 V/microsecond; and<br>   (e)    a typical power dissipation of more than 10 mW per amplifier, if the typical slew rate at unity-gain exceeds 2.5 V/microsecond;<br>  (5)   untuned alternating current (AC) amplifiers, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a bandwidth of less than 3 MHz; and<br>   (b)    a maximum rated power dissipation of 5 W or less at an ambient temperature of 298 K (25°C);<br> (l)  analogue multiplier or divider monolithic integrated circuits, multichip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   a best-case rated linearity of no better than ±0.5 per cent of full scale; and<br>  (2)   a −3 dB small signal bandwidth of no more than 1 MHz;<br> (m)  converter monolithic integrated circuits, multichip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, the following:<br>  (1)   analogue-to-digital converters, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum conversion rate to rated accuracy of no more than 50,000 complete conversions per second, ie a conversion time to maximum resolution of no less than 20 micro-second; and<br>   (b)    an accuracy of no better than ±0.025 per cent of full scale over the specified operating temperature range;<br>  (2)   analogue-to-digital converters, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    designed for digital voltmeter applications; and<br>   (b)    permitting characteristics corresponding to those of instruments specifically excluded from head (f) of the entry IL1529 in this Group;<br>  (3)   digital-to-analogue converters, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum settling time to rated linearity of no less than:<br>    (i)     5 microseconds for voltage output converters; or<br>    (ii)     250 ns for current output converters; and<br>   (b)    non-linearity of equal to or worse than ±0.025 per cent of full scale over the specified operating temperature range;<br>  (4)   voltage (rms-to-DC) converters;<br>  (5)   voltage-to-frequency converters having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    not employing delta or delta/sigma modulation techniques;<br>   (b)    a rated accuracy of no better than ±0.01 per cent of full scale; and<br>   (c)    a gain drift of no less than ±50 × 10<sup>−6</sup>/K at rated frequency;<br>   Note: “Gain drift” means the maximum change in gain over a specified temperature range.<br> (n)  interface monolithic integrated circuits, multichip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, the following:<br>  (1)   line drivers and line receivers having a typical propagation delay time from data input to output of not less than 15 ns;<br>  (2)   peripheral or display drivers, having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum rated output current of 500 mA or less;<br>   (b)    a typical propagation delay time from data input to output of not less than 20 ns; and<br>   (c)    a maximum rated output voltage of 80 V or less;<br>  (3)   sense amplifiers, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a typical propagation delay time from data input to output of not less than 15 ns; and<br>   (b)    a typical input threshold voltage of not less than 10 mV;<br>  (4)   storage or clock drivers, having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum rated output current of 500 mA or less;<br>   (b)    a maximum rated output voltage of 30 V or less; and<br>   (c)    a typical propagation delay time from data input to output of not less than 20 ns;<br> (o)  peripheral positive-channel type or negative channel type metal-oxide-seminconductor (PMOS or NMOS) monolithic integrated circuits or multichip integrated circuits, designed only for:<br>  (1)   the support of microprocessor microcircuits which are excluded by exception (2)(D)(i) to head (d) above; and<br>  (2)   having any of the following functions:<br>   (a)    parallel input/output controller (PIO);<br>   (b)    serial input/output controller (SIO);<br>   (c)    dual asynchronous receiver/transmitter (DART); or<br>   (d)    counter/timer circuit (CTC);<br> (p)  sample and hold monolithic integrated circuits, hybrid integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or multichip integrated circuits, having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   an acquisition time of not less than 10 microseconds; and<br>  (2)   a non-linearity, of equal to or worse than ±0.01 per cent of full scale for a hold time of 1 microsecond;<br> (q)  timing monolithic integrated circuits, hybrid integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or multichip integrated circuits, having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   a typical timing error of not less than ±0.5 per cent; and<br>  (2)   a typical rise time of not less than 100 ns;<br> (r)  voltage monolithic integrated circuits, multichip integrated circuits, film type integrated circuits or hybrid integrated circuits, the following—<br>  (1)   voltage comparators, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum input offset voltage of not less than 2 mV; and<br>   (b)    a typical switching speed (ie typical response time) of not less than 30 ns;<br>  (2)   voltage references, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a rated accuracy of not better than ±0.1 per cent; and<br>   (b)    a temperature coefficient of the voltage of not less than 15 × 10<sup>−6</sup>/K;<br>  (3)   linear type voltage regulators, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a rated nominal output voltage of 50 V or less; and<br>   (b)    a maximum output current of 2A or less;<br>  (4)   switching type voltage regulators, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a rated nominal output voltage of 40 V or less; and<br>   (b)    a maximum output current of 150 mA or less;<br>  Note: For voltage regulators, the 358 K (85°C) upper temperature limit specified in exception (2)(B) to head (d) above is not applicable.<br> (s)  non-coherent light-emitting alphanumeric displays, which do not incorporate other monolithic integrated circuits;<br> (t)  non-coherent light-emitting alphanumeric displays, which incorporate monolithic integrated circuits having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   used for decoding, controlling or driving the display; and<br>  (2)   not integral with the actual display device;<br> (u)  simple encapsulated photocoupler (transopter) optical integrated circuits, having both of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   electrical input and output; and<br>  (2)   any incorporated light-emitting diodes can emit only non-coherent light;<br>(3) unencapsulated integrated circuits, having all of the following characteristics—<br>(A) based exclusively upon silicon;<br>(B) not designed or rated as radiation hardened; and<br>(C) being any of the following types:<br> (a)  bipolar monolithic integrated circuits (save complex custom-built bipolar digital monolithic integrated circuits) having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   designed to perform a single digital logic function or a combination of digital logic functions;<br>  (2)   a basic gate propagation delay time of not less than 5 ns;<br>  (3)   a product of the basic gate propagation delay time and the basic gate power dissipation per gate of net less than 70 pJ; and<br>  (4)   no more than 24 input/output pads;<br> (b)  bipolar monolithic integrated circuits (save complex custom-built bipolar digital monolithic integrated circuits) having all of following characteristics:<br>  (1)   designed for operation in civil applications;<br>  (2)   being either:<br>   (a)    electronic switches, externally controlled by inductive, magnetic or optical means; or<br>   (b)    threshold value switches;<br>  (3)   with switching times of 0.5 microsecond or more; and<br>  (4)   having no more than 24 input/output pads;<br> (c)  monolithic integrated circuits having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   no user-accessible microprogrammability;<br>  (2)   designed for and by virtue of circuit design limited to use in civil radio or television receivers;<br>  (3)   rated for operation at 11 MHz or less;<br>  (4)   not designed for station scanning applications;<br>  (5)   not utilizing charge-coupled device (CCD) technology;<br>  (6)   not intended for beam lead bonding; and<br>  (7)   if intended for video or luminance amplifiers, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum rated supply voltage not exceeding 30 V; and<br>   (b)    a typical bandwidth not exceeding 7.5MHz;<br> (d)  monolithic integrated circuits having all of the following characteristics:<br>  (1)   no user-accessible microprogrammability;<br>  (2)   not utilizing charge-coupled device (CCD) technology;<br>  (3)   not intended for beam lead bonding; and<br>  (4)   designed or programmed by the manufacturer for one or more of the following applications only:<br>   (a)    timekeeping applications, including watches or clocks; or<br>   (b)    cardiac pacemakers or hearing aids;<br> (e)  amplifier monolithic integrated circuits, the following:<br>  (1)   audio amplifiers, having a maximum rated power ouput of 25 W or less at an ambient temperature of 298 K (25°C);<br>  (2)   operational amplifiers, having all of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a typical unity-gain open-loop bandwidth of not more than 5 MHz;<br>   (b)    a typical open-loop voltage gain of not more than 562,000 ie 115 dB;<br>   (c)    a maximum intrinsic rated input offset voltage of not less than 2.5 mV; and<br>   (d)    a typical slew rate at unity-gain not exceeding 2.5 V/microsecond;<br> (f)  voltage monolithic integrated circuits, the following:<br>  (1)   voltage comparators, having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a maximum input offset voltage of not less than 5 mV; and<br>   (b)    a typical switching speed (ie typical response time) of not less than 50 ns;<br>  (2)   linear type voltage regulators having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a rated nominal output voltage of 40 V or less; and<br>   (b)    a maximum output current of 1A or less;<br>  (3)   switching type voltage regulators having both of the following characteristics:<br>   (a)    a rated nominal output voltage of 40 V or less; and<br>   (b)    a maximum output current of 150 mA or less;<br>  (4)   encapsulated integrated circuits having all of the following characteristics—<br>   (a)    not designed or rated as radiation hardened;<br>   (b)    not rated for operation at an ambient temperature below 233 K (−40°C) or above 358 K (85°C);<br>   (c)    packaged in hermetically sealed ceramic packages which are specifically expected from head (b) of this entry; and<br>   (d)    containing unencapsulated integrated circuits which are specifically excepted by exception (3) of head (d) of this entry.</td><td></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td> (dd)  Technological documents the information in which relates to the manufacture of thin film passive networks</td><td>D</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td>In this entry—<br>“assembly” means a number of electronic components (eg circuit elements, discrete components, integrated circuits, etc) connected together to perform a specific function, replaceable as an entity and normally capable of being disassembled;<br>“basic gate power dissipation” means the power dissipation value corresponding to the basic gate utilized within a family of monolithic integrated circuits. This may be specified, for a given family, either as the power dissipation per typical gate or as the typical power dissipation per gate;<br>“basic gate propagation delay time” means the propagation delay time value corresponding to the basic gate utilized within a family of monolithic integrated circuits. This may be specified, for a given family, either as the propagation delay time per typical gate or as the typical propagation delay time per gate;<br>“circuit element” means a single active or passive functional part of an electronic circuit, such as one diode, one transistor, one resistor, one capacitor, etc;<br>“discrete component” means a separately packaged circuit element with its own external connections;<br>“film type integrated circuit” means an array of circuit elements and metallic interconnections formed by deposition of a thick or thin film on an insulating substrate;<br>“hybrid integrated circuit” means any combination of integrated circuits, circuit elements or discrete components connected together to perform a specific function;<br>“manufacturer” means the individual or organization designing an integrated circuit or a programmed for an intended application, in contrast to an individual or organization merely programming an integrated circuit at, or in accordance with, a user’s request;<br>“microcomputer microcircuit” means a monolithic integrated circuit or multichip integrated circuit containing an arithmetic logic unit (ALU) capable of executing general purpose instructions from an internal storage; on data contained in the internal storage; (the internal storage may be augmented by an external storage.)<br>“microprocessor microcircuit” means a monolithic integrated circuit or multichip integrated circuit containing an arithmetic logic unit (ALU) capable of executing a series of general purpose instructions from an external storage; (the microprocessor microcircuit normally does not contain integral user-accessible storage, although storage present on-the-chip may be used in performing its logic function.)<br>“module” means a number of electronic components (eg circuit elements, discrete components, integrated circuits etc) connected together to perform a specific function, replaceable as an entity and not normally capable of being disassembled;<br>“monolithic integrated circuit” means a combination of passive or active circuit elements or both which:<br>(a) is formed by means of diffusion processes, implantation processes or deposition processes in or on a single semiconducting piece of material, a so-called “chip”;<br>(b) can be considered as indivisibly associated; and<br>(c) perform the function of a circuit;<br>“multichip integrated circuit” means two or more monolithic integrated circuits bonded to a common substrate;<br>“optical integrated circuit” means a monolithic integrated circuit or a hybrid integrated circuit, containing one or more parts designed to function as a photosensor or photoemitter or to perform an optical or electro-optical function;<br>“speed” means the time to fetch an operand C and another operand D, both from an external storage outside any work register, add these operands and put the result back in storage. The addressing mode which yields the shortest execution time shall be used. The result of the add operation shall be stored in either the same location as one of the addends or in some other location. This choice shall be made to give the shortest execution time at the highest specified clock frequency;<br>“speed-power dissipation product” means the product of the speed and the typical power dissipation which shall be taken at the clock frequency used in the speed computation. The typical power dissipation must be the lowest of the following:<br>(a) the specified typical internal power dissipation;<br>(b) one half the maximum internal power dissipation;<br>(c) the product of the nominal supply voltage and typical total supply current; or<br>(d) one half of the product of the nominal supply voltage and maximum total supply current;<br>“substrate” means a sheet of base material with or without an interconnection pattern and on which or within which discrete components, integrated circuits or both can be located;<br>“user-accessible microprogrammability” means the facility allowing a user to insert, modify or replace microprogrammes;<br>“user-accessible programmability” means the facility allowing a user to insert, modify or replace programmes by means other than:<br>(a) a physical change in wiring or interconnections; or<br>(b) the setting of function controls including entry of parameters.</td><td></td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

#### GROUP 3G — Electronic Equipment including Computers, Software and Telecommunications, and Photographic Equipment

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| IL1565 | Electronic computers, related equipment, equipment or systems containing electronic computers, and technology therefor, the following: and specially designed components and accessories for such electronic computers and related equipment— |  |
|  | - (a) Analogue computers, and related equipment therefor, which are designed or modified for use in ariborne vehicles, missiles or space vehicles and rated for continuous operation at temperatures from below 228 K (−45°C) to above 328 K (+55°C) | C |
|  | - (b) Equipment or systems containing analogue computers specified in head (a) above | C |
|  | - (c) Analogue computers and related equipment therefor, other than those specified in head (a) above | C |
|  | - except—<br>  - (i) those which neither—<br>    - (a) are capable of containing more than 20 summers, integrators, multipliers or function generators; nor<br>    - (b) have facilities for readily varying the interconnections of such components;<br>  - (ii) those which have all the following characteristics—<br>    - (a) they use neither:<br>    - (1)  optical computation devices; nor<br>    - (2)  acoustic wave devices specified in the entry IL1586 in Group 3G;<br>    - (d) the rated errors for summers, inverters and integrators are not less than:<br>    - (1)  static : 0.01%<br>    - (2)  total at 1 kHz: 0.15%<br>    - (g) the rated errors for multipliers are not less than:<br>    - (1)  static : 0.25%<br>    - (2)  total at kHz: 0.25%<br>    - (j) the rated errors for fixed function generators (log and sine/cosine) are not less than: static : 0.1%<br>    - (k) they have no more than 350 operational amplifiers; and<br>    - (l) they have more than four integrator time scales switchable during one programme.<br>- NOTE<br>  - 1. The percentage for (ii)(b)(1) above applies to the actual output voltage; all the other percentages apply to full scale, that is from maximum negative to maximum positive reference voltages.<br>  - 2. Total errors at 1 kHz for (ii)(b)(2) and (ii)(c)(2) above are to be measured with those resistors incorporated in the inverter, summer or integrator which provide the least error.<br>  - 3. Total error measurements include all errors of the unit resulting from, for example, tolerances of resistors and capacitors, tolerances of input and output impedances of amplifiers, the effects of loading, the effects of phase shift or the generating of functions. |  |
|  | - (d) Hybrid computers and related equipment therefor, having all the following characteristics— | C |
|  | - (1) the analogue section is specified in head (c) above;<br>- (2) the digital section has an internal fixed or alterable storage of more than 2,048 bit; and<br>- (3) facilities are included for processing numerical data from the analogue section in the digital section or vice versa; |  |
|  | - (e) Analogue computers specified in heads (a) to (c) above (inclusive) or digital computers containing equipment for interconnecting analogue computers with digital computers | C |
|  | - (f) Digital computers and related equipment therefor, with any of the following characteristics— |  |
|  | - (1) designed or modified for use in airborne vehicles, missiles or space vehicles and rated for continuous operation at temperatures from below 228 K (−45°C) to above 328 K (+55°C) | C |
|  | - (2) designed or modified to limit electromagnetic radiation to levels much less than those required by government civil interference specifications | C |
|  | - (3) designed as ruggedized or radiation-hardened equipment and capable of meeting military specifications for ruggedized or radiation-hardened equipment | C |
|  | - (4) modified for military use, or | C |
|  | - (5) designed or modified for certifiable multi-level security or certifiable user isolation applicable to government classified material or to applications requiring an equivalent level of security | C |
|  | - (g) Equipment or systems containing digital computers specified in head (f) above | C |
|  | - (h) Digital computers and related equipment therefor, (other than those specified in heads (e) and (f) above even when embedded in, incorporated in, or associated with equipment or systems, and other than digital computers embedded in equipment described elsewhere in this Schedule), including but not limited to the following— | C |
|  | - (1) Digital computers and related equipment designed or modified for— |  |
|  | - (a) signal processing | C |
|  | - (b) image enhancement | C |
|  | - (c) local area networks | C |
|  | - (d) multi-data-stream processing | C |
|  | - (e) combined recognition, understanding and interpretation of image, continuous (connected) speech or connected word text other than signal processing or image enhancement | C |
|  | - (f) real time processing of sensor data having both of the following characteristics: | C |
|  | -  (1)  concerning events occurring outside the computer using facility; and<br>-  (2)  provided by equipment specified in entry IL1501, IL1502, IL1510 or IL1518 in Group 3F. |  |
|  | - (g) microprocessor or microcomputer development systems | C |
|  | - (h) fault tolerance | C |
|  | - (j) user-accessible microprogrammability except where this facility is limited to— | C |
|  | - except where this facility is limited to—<br>-  (a)  loading, reloading or inserting of micro-programmes provided by the supplier; or<br>-  (b)  simple loading of microprogrammes which may or may not be provided by the supplier, but which are neither designed to be accessible to the user nor accompanied by training or software for user accessibility. |  |
|  | - (k) data (message) switching | C |
|  | - (l) stored programme controlled circuit switching, or | C |
|  | - (m) wide area networks | C |
|  | - (2) digital computers and related equipment therefor, having both the following characteristics— | C |
|  | - (a) size, weight, power consumption and reliability or other characteristics (eg bubble memory), which allow easy application in mobile tactical military systems; and<br>- (b) ruggedised above the level required for a normal commercial/office environment, but not necessarily up to levels specified in head (f) above; |  |
|  | - There shall be excluded from head (h)—<br>  - (i) digital computers and related equipment therefor, provided that—<br>  - (a)  they are embedded in other equipment or systems; or they do not include related equipment specified elsewhere in this Schedule other than input/output control unit-disk drive combinations described in exception (iiA) below to this head;<br>  - (b)  they are not the principal element of the other equipment or systems in which they are embedded;<br>  - (c)  the other equipment or systems are not specified elsewhere in this Schedule;<br>  - (d)  they have been designed and used for non-strategic applications;<br>  - (e)  they are by nature of design or performance restricted to the particular application for which they have been designed;<br>  - (f)  the total processing data rate of any one embedded digital computer does not exceed 54 million bit per second;<br>  - (g)  the sum of the total processing data rate of each embedded digital computer does not exceed 100 million bit per second;<br>  - (h)  they do not include equipment or systems specified in sub-head (a)(2) of the entry IL1519 in Group 3F or by the entry IL1567 in this Group; and<br>  - (j)  they do not include equipment specified in sub-head (h)(1) other than equipment for:<br>  - (1)   signal processing or image enhancement which lacks user-accessible programmability and is embedded in medical imaging equipment; or<br>  - (2)   local area networks not specified elsewhere in this Schedule.<br>  - (ii) digital computers, and related equipment therefor, provided that—<br>  - (a)  they are incorporated in other equipment or systems;<br>  - (b)  they are not the principal element of the other equipment or systems in which they are incorporated;<br>  - (c)  the other equipment or systems are not specified elsewhere in this Schedule;<br>  - (d)  the total processing data rate of any one incorporated digital computer does not exceed 28 million bit per second;<br>  - (e)  the total internal storage available to the user does not exceed 9.8 million bit;<br>  - (f)  they do not include related equipment specified elsewhere in this Schedule other than input/output control unit-disk drive combinations described in exception (iiA) below to this head;<br>  - (g)  they do not include equipment or systems specified in sub-head (a)(2) of the entry IL1519 in Group 3F or in entry IL1567 in this Group;<br>  - (h)  they do not include equipment specified in sub-head (h)(2) above;<br>  - (j)  they do not include equipment specified in sub-head (h)(1) other than equipment for:<br>  - (1)   signal processing or image enhancement which lacks user-accessible programmability and is embedded in medical imaging equipment; or<br>  - (2)   local area networks not specified elsewhere in this Schedule.<br>  - (iiA) digital computers or related equipment therefore, provided that—<br>  - (a)  where there is incorporated or associated in input/output control unit-disk drive combination, it has all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (1)   total transfer rate not exceeding 5.5 million bit per second;<br>  - (2)   total connected net capacity not exceeding 320 million bit;<br>  - (3)   no more than two independent drives; and<br>  - (4)   total access rate not exceeding 80 accesses per second with a maximum access rate of 40 accesses per second per drive;<br>  - (b)  the goods in which digital computers or related equipment are incorporated or with which digital computers or related equipment are associated are excluded from head (h) of this entry by exception (i) or (ii) above.<br>  - (iii) digital computers (other than those specified in sub-head (h)(1) above) and related equipment, having all the following characteristics—<br>  - (a)  they are complete systems;<br>  - (b)  they are designed and announced by the manufacturer for an identifiable civil use;<br>  - (c)  they are not specially designed for any equipment specified elsewhere in this Schedule;<br>  - (d)  they have a total processing data rate not exceeding 6.5 million bit per second;<br>  - (e)  the total internal storage available to the user does not exceed 6.2 million bit; and<br>  - (f)  they do not include a central processing unit implemented with more than two microprocessor or microcomputer micro-circuits other than any dedicated micro-processor microcircuit;<br>  - (g)  they do not include a microprocessor or microcomputer microcircuit with more than 16 bit word length or a bus architecture with more than 16 bit;<br>  - (h)  they do not include analogue-to-digital or digital-to-analogue converter microcircuits specified in the entry IL1568, except in the case of direct driven video monitors for normal commercial television;<br>  - (j)  they do not include related equipment specified elsewhere in this Schedule other than input/output control unit-disk drive combination having all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (xlii)   total transfer rate not exceeding 5.5 million bit per second;<br>  - (2)   total connected net capacity not exceeding 200 million bit;<br>  - (3)   no more than one independent drive; and<br>  - (4)   total access rate not exceeding 40 accesses per second; and<br>  - (k)  they do not include equipment specified in sub-head (a)(2) of entry IL1519 in Group 3F or in entry IL1567 in this Group.<br>  - (iv) peripheral equipment, provided it lacks user-accessible programmability, the following—<br>  - (a)  card punches and readers;<br>  - (b)  paper tape punches and readers;<br>  - (c)  manually operated keyboards and teletype devices;<br>  - (d)  manually operated graphic tablets not having more than 1,024 resolvable points along any axis;<br>  - (e)  impact printers;<br>  - (f)  non-impact printers, not specified in head (b) or (c) of entry IL1572 in this Group, which does not exceed:<br>  - (1)   2,000 lines (30 pages) per minute; or<br>  - (2)   600 characters per second;<br>  - (g)  plotting equipment, not specified in head (b) or (c) of entry IL1572 in this Group, producing a physical record by ink, photographic, thermal, or electrostatic techniques, and which has:<br>  - (1)   a linear accuracy worse than or equal to ±0.004%; and<br>  - (2)   an active plotting area less than or equal to 1,700mm by 1,300mm;<br>  - (h)  digitising equipment generating rectilinear co-ordinate data by manual or semi-automatic tracing of physical records, which has:<br>  - (1)   a linear accuracy worse than or equal to ±0.004 per cent; and<br>  - (2)   an active digitising area less than or equal to 1,700mm by 1,300mm;<br>  - (j)  optical mark recognition (OMR) equipment;<br>  - (k)  optical character recognition (OCR) equipment which:<br>  - (1)   does not contain signal processing or image enhancement equipment;
