and<br>  - (2)   is only for:<br>  - (i)    stylised OCR characters;<br>  - (ii)    other internationally standardised stylized character fonts; or<br>  - (iii)    other non-stylised or hand printed numerics and up to 10 hand printed alphabetic or other characters;<br>  - (l)  displays or monitors having all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (1)   not including equipment specified in sub-head (h)(2) above;<br>  - (2)   not containing cathode ray tubes specified in entry IL1541 in Group 4;<br>  - (3)   if capable of other than alpha-numeric characters, graphs and symbols in fixed formats having all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (i)    not more than 1,024 resolvable elements along any axis;<br>  - (ii)    (except in the case of direct driven video monitors), not more than 16 shades of grey or colour; and<br>  - (iii)    (except in the case of direct driven video monitors) the maximum bit transfer rate from the electronic computer to the display does not exceed 19,200 bit per second,<br>  - (m)  displays or monitors having all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (1)   they do not contain cathode ray tubes;<br>  - (2)   they are not capable of displaying more than 3 levels namely off, intermediate and full on; and<br>  - (3)   they do not have as an integral part of the display device:<br>  - (a)    circuitry; or<br>  - (b)    non-mechanical character generation devices;<br>  - (n)  displays having all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (1)   not containing cathode ray tubes specified in the entry IL1541 in Group 3F;<br>  - (2)   being part of industrial or medical equipment; and<br>  - (3)   not specially designed for use with electronic computers;<br>  - (o)  graphic displays specially designed for signature or security checking having an active display area not exceeding 150 sq cm;<br>  - (q)  light gun devices or other manual graphic input devices which are:<br>  - (1)   part of displays not specified elsewhere in this Schedule; and<br>  - (2)   limited to 1,024 resolvable elements along any axis;<br>  - (r)  disk drives for non-rigid magnetic media (floppy disks) which do not exceed:<br>  - (1)   a gross capacity of 17 million bit;<br>  - (2)   a maximum bit transfer rate of 0.52 million bit per second; or<br>  - (3)   an access rate of 12 accesses per second;<br>  - (s)  cassette/cartridge tape drives or magnetic tape drives which do not exceed:<br>  - (1)   a maximum bit packing density of 131 bit per mm per track; or<br>  - (2)   a maximum bit transfer rate of 2.66 million bit per second;<br>  - (xcvii) input/output interface or control units, provided that they lack user-accessible programmability, the following—<br>  - (a)  designed for use with peripheral equipment excluded from head (h) by exception (iv) above;<br>  - (b)  designed for use with digital recording or reproducing equipment specially designed to use magnetic card, tag, label or bank cheque recording media, excluded by exception (ii) to head (a) of entry IL1572 in this Group; or<br>  - (c)  designed to meet ANSI/IEEE Standard 488-1978 or IEC Publication 625-1;<br>  - (vi) equipment for local area networks which do not have any of the following characteristics—<br>  - (a)  interfaces and protocols exceeding layer 2 of the Open System Interconnection (OSI) reference model, that is ISO logical link control Draft International Standard (DIS) 8802/2, IEEE 802.2, 802.3, 802.4, 802.5, or equivalents;<br>  - (b)  implementations that contain functions of, or equivalent to those provided by, CCITT X.25, Level 3, protocols or above;<br>  - (c)  maximum data signalling rate on the common transmission medium of more than 2 million bits per second; or<br>  - (d)  inter network gateways;<br>  - (vii) personal computers and related equipment therefor, not excluded by exclusions (i) to (vi) above, provided they meet all the following conditions—<br>  - (a)  they are not specified in head (h) above;<br>  - (b)  they are exported as complete systems;<br>  - (c)  if they are stand-alone graphic workstations they do not have all of the following characteristics:<br>  - (i)   they are stand-alone graphics workstations designed or modified for the generation, transformation, and display of 2 or 3 dimensional vectors;<br>  - (ii)   they have a total processing data rate of the central processing unit exceeding 28 million bit per second;<br>  - (iii)   they have a central processing unit with a word length exceeding 16-bit; and<br>  -   (NOTE: Microprocessor based systems with 16-bit word-length and not more than a 32-bit architecture are regarded as 16-bit systems for the purpose of sub-paragraph (iii))<br>  - (iv)   they exceed either of the following limits:<br>  - (a)    block move data rate- 800,000 pixels/sec; or<br>  - (b)    maximum bit transfer rate of the channel for direct access to the main storage (Direct Memory Access or DMA channel)-11 million bit per second;<br>  -   NOTE: sub-paragraph (c) does not apply to workstations designed for and limited to graphic arts (eg printing, publishing).<br>  - (d)  they are not ruggedized above the level required for a normal commercial/office environment;<br>- (j) Technology, the following—<br>  - (1) technology applicable to the— |  |
|  | - (i) development, production or use of electronic computers or related equipment, even if these electronic computers or related equipment are not specified in this entry | D |
|  | - except—<br>-  (a)  technology which is unique to related equipment excluded under sub-heads (h)(2)(iv)(a) to (c), (e), (f), (m), (n) or (q) above and which is not specified elsewhere in this Schedule;<br>-  (b)  the minimum technology necessary for the use of electronic computers or related equipment not specified elsewhere in this entry; or |  |
|  | - (ii) development, production or use of equipment or systems specified in head (b) or (g) of this entry | D |
|  | - For the purpose of this subhead “use” means assembly, operation, maintenance or repair. |  |

|  | - (i) electronic computers or related equipment specified in this entry into other equipment or systems whether or not the other equipment or systems are specified in this Schedule; except technology for integration which is unique to the other equipment or systems provided they are not specified elsewhere in this Schedule or | D |
